# 2011 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** circles · hangout
**Star:** Google+ Hangout · `itt11-gplus` · `sites/googleplus/index.html`
**ILS June:** 346,004,403 · 2,282,955,130
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 313
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt11-gplus`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2011/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2011/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2011 — dual scale · bans
2. Google+ — Circles · Hangout
3. Spotify US — invite
4. Siri — 4S
5. Timeline — memoir
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt11-gplus`

**Room:** `years/2011/sites/googleplus/index.html`

**Nostalgia:** Google+ Hangout — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**

**Complete writes**

- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-gplus` — do not write `itt10-*` or `itt12-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Spotify US → `years/2011/sites/spotify/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

### L-0001 · `itt11-airbnb-lx` · sites/airbnb/about.html

**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `google` · `youtube` · `facebook`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="airbnb-lx"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt11-airbnb-lx']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + fewer than 2 picks + Save → never writes.
7. `itt11-gplus` stays empty. `itt10-airbnb-lx` and `itt12-airbnb-lx` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click 2 distinct `[data-lo-pick]` rows.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="airbnb-lx"]`.
- `localStorage['itt11-airbnb-lx']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2011", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt11-airbnb-lx`.
- Does **not** write `itt11-gplus`.
